The materials here are roughly sorted and roughly written. They are not exactly “roughly thought through” so much as “rough-and-readily thought through.”

This is a working archive, not a polished paper. It contains native notes, AI-assisted drafts, speculative branches, formalization attempts, prediction records, critique cycles, visual aids, and process artifacts. Not every file is equally central. Not every claim is equally mature. Some documents preserve false starts or abandoned branches because those are part of the history.

Introduction to the Archive
This is primarily a personal archive and indexing system, built so I can access and track my own files more easily. That said, the underlying idea is public: anyone is welcome to consider it, criticize it, share it, fork it, test it, or ignore it.

The project started small. It grew because once the original ontological commitments were established, many later structures seemed to fall into place naturally and intuitively. The archive records that development: the intuitions, the translations, the overextensions, the corrections, the audits, the prediction attempts, and the ongoing effort to separate what is solid from what is speculative.

The archive should be read as a theory-building record, not as a finished theory.

SAT = Scalar–Angular Theory / Scalar–Angular–Torsion Theory
Earlier SAT = Stringing-Along Theory.

The original name was informal and partly self-deprecating. The later names reflect the framework’s central geometric vocabulary: scalar structure, angular interaction, and torsion/twist in four-dimensional worldline geometry.

Core Idea
SAT begins from a simple geometric reversal:

The real physical object is not the point-particle as seen at an instant. The real physical object is the extended four-dimensional worldline.

SAT calls these physicalized worldlines filaments.

A particle, as we observe it, is the intersection between a filament and a moving/resolving time surface.

Fundamental Intuitions of SAT
0th —
the real world is the world of particle worldlines plotted in four dimensions
this world is a hypercomplex tangled Zottenwelt of worldlines
SAT calls these worldlines filaments
1st —
time moves like a wavefront through these worldlines
SAT calls this wavefront the time surface
2nd —
a fundamental particle is the intersection of a filament with the time surface
3rd —
there must be a real interaction between the time surface and the filaments
this interaction involves energy transfer
4th —
filament/time-surface interactions govern particle formation and stability
5th —
filament/time-surface interactions exert force back on the time surface
6th —
back-transmission from the filament network curves the time surface
7th —
filaments and the time surface are physical, not merely conceptual
forces may propagate along a filament’s four-dimensional extent
“backbleed” effects are structurally self-limiting
8th —
known deviations from SM, GR, or QM may be explainable through SAT geometry
9th —
particle properties may arise from intersection geometry
θ₄ = filament/time-surface interaction angle
early SAT treated θ₄ as a primary mass contributor
10th —
physical constants should emerge from SAT geometry or phenomena arising from it
Pillars and Propositions
Pillar 1 —
the Standard Model describes the evolving three-dimensional structure of matter
SAT treats SM structures as importable unless evidence or internal logic forces revision
SM behavior is translated into filament/time-surface geometry
Proposition A —
the Standard Model is treated as essentially accurate unless forced otherwise
Pillar 2 —
the back-pull of the filament structure on the time surface recreates curved spacetime
gravity emerges from time-surface curvature induced by filament structure
Proposition B —
General Relativity is treated as essentially correct unless forced otherwise
Proposition C —
backbleed effects are limited by filament and time-surface geometry
causality violations are structurally constrained
quantum-scale weirdness may reflect limited cross-slice effects
Pillar 3 —
Quantum Mechanics is treated as essentially correct unless forced otherwise
QM is translated into the geometry of SAT rather than discarded
Prime General Prediction Pathway —
cross-temporal tensions are not explicitly accounted for in SM, GR, or QM
this blind spot may produce observable anomalies
Pillar 4 —
filament behavior matters, not only filament/time-surface angle
vibrating-string, coil, twist, and locking analogies are used as geometric scaffolds
particle laws may correspond to repeatable intersection traces
QCD-like behavior may arise from two-part and three-part geometric locking
Proposition D —
force carriers may be structural transitions rather than persistent filaments
light is modeled as an excitation/ripple in the filament network
Proposition E —
vacuum and matter may be different regimes of the same filament/time-surface system
vacuum may correspond to near-aligned filaments with low energy transfer
matter may correspond to higher-misalignment filament intersections
Key Insight —
vacuum and matter lie on one continuum of filament/time-surface interaction
Proposition F —
some forms of String Theory may be partial mappings of filament/time-surface geometry
SAT may import useful string-theoretic structures by translation rather than identity
Development Timeline
Terminology is defined only when useful. Later entries use established SAT terms unless the meaning changes.

|| NEEDS + FIXES || 1 GLASS+HSH >> 2 WORKSHOP >> 3 LIVE >> 4 ARTIFACTS >> 5 WEBSITE >> [[NATHAN]]

Formal-verification intake desk | 26 October 2026
This is an intake register, not a completed scientific showroom. The issue descriptions below document bounded Lean mathematical checks and original-source evidence. They do not by themselves establish H(s)H physical models, new physics, measured predictions, theorem-scope completeness, or LIVE/WEBSITE publication. Preserve the original Factory Floor chain: 1 GLASS+HSH → 2 WORKSHOP → 3 LIVE → 4 ARTIFACTS → 5 WEBSITE.

Entry	Source / checked mathematical content	Lean CI readback	Current Factory state
EQ-0001 / issue #2	Three equal sphere carrier radius identities and selected algebraic endpoint/inequality statements; embedded regular-circle topology not fully proved in Lean	run 39987817298: verify success	INTAKE / eligible for candidacy consideration only
EQ-0002 / issue #3	Isovolumetric deformation exponential/trace identities; constitutive dynamics not derived	run 39996929325: verify success	INTAKE / mathematically checked
EQ-0003 / issue #1	Historical filament dispersion equation algebraic rearrangement only, not field-action proof	run 39960043562: verify success	INTAKE / theory unvalidated
EQ-0004 / issue #4	Finite-dimensional/action field Hamiltonian Legendre identities; not a selected H(s)H action	run 40005392966: verify success	INTAKE / theory unvalidated
EQ-0005 / issue #5	Classical linear-wave/action and ring-mode algebra; no H(s)H finite-core constitutive coefficients or held-out observable derived	run 40040647137: verify success	INTAKE / theory unvalidated
EQ-0006 / issue #14	SAT.O13 scalar-times-unitary norm gate; loop-dressed mass matrix and A4-to-three-flavor projection not formalized	run 41330715937: success, 8,945 jobs	INTAKE / algebraic check only; not an H(s)H candidate
At the 26 October inspection, the shared HsH formal-verification pull request #262 reported open, merged=false, mergeable=false. The individual verified issue reports identify original SAT archive source paths and committed proof variants. GitHub Actions job verify status was independently fetched for all five runs then cited. That constitutes CI outcome readback, not line-by-line proof-body adjudication; do that in the WORKSHOP.

Before a showroom/public-facing promotion: independently open every exact Lean proof file and matching historical raw-source context; verify inputs, hypotheses, theorem statement, no hidden axioms/sorry, toolchain and reproducibility, external physical-data status, source/candidate separation, and editorial/presentation routing. Retain false-starts and negative gates. A finished showroom should link exact proof SHAs and explain explicitly what the theorem did not establish.

Current project-return route: HsH Phase Steering, S04. This is a front-door index over existing issues, not an independent assertion of theorem validity.

Follow-up direct proof-source reading, 26 October 2026
Read all five exact source proof files from HsH branch infra/lean4-verification-20261023: ArchiveEQ0001.lean blob 12f52f9530e3d73a23f33aac99c775f2df236e91; ArchiveEQ0002.lean blob f98ef92a5f62b60818d8d78f952f23f5d2e4d8fa; ArchiveEQ0003.lean blob e126892ff8912771c7aee5da0cdd0d64f5d2fc80; ArchiveEQ0004.lean blob 1726467eefab4ca44cfb7dd366069ee02660bcd3; ArchiveEQ0005.lean blob 142e8ff5a037a4508b581bf146913f070e1f894d.

Scope audit: All five fetched Lean sources contain theorem proofs using library tactics rather than sorry, new axiom declarations or admit. This is source-code inspection coupled to previously verified CI run outcomes; it does not establish that every theorem in imported Mathlib or historical source is checked here. The actual theorem statements make their needed input equations explicit as hypotheses. EQ-0001 proves real-number radius relations, not existence of embedded intersection topology; EQ-0002 proves real exponential identities, not finite-strain dynamics; EQ-0003 rearranges assumed dispersion; EQ-0004 performs finite-dimensional Legendre identities; EQ-0005 proves assumed local balance algebra and dispersion/zero-mode identities, not Euler–Lagrange derivation, independently physical coefficients or observed data.

First historical-source follow-up: inspected SAT archive SAT O Derivations/CODE-LOCKED DERIVS.txt D2, lines 21–114, source blob 783b05d39d59bf854b92090e15d205e6823b51c8. The source writes periodic k_n=2πn/L and omega_n=|k_n|sqrt(T/m), then includes factors 1/sqrt(2*m*omega_n) in the field-operator expansion without an explicit n≠0 restriction in that displayed sum. Its n=0 mode makes the denominator zero. Standard mathematical resolution requires excluding the zero mode from those oscillator sums or treating the constant/center-of-mass mode separately. Mark D2 quantum-mode expansion REQUIRES_ZERO_MODE_TREATMENT pending full intended physical/quantum source check, not Lean disproved or physically falsified. Related issue #5 already proves mu≠0, mu*omega²=0 ⇒ omega=0, but it does not formalize the source's singular 1/sqrt(omega) coefficient or correct it. Distinguish m and linear-density mu as noted in existing intake.

Next WORKSHOP test: isolate precisely the physical 1D density and the zero-mode Hilbert-space construction; verify source derivational obligations around mode normalization and canonical commutation relations, without invented physical measurements. Any new physical calculation must meet current real-system observational-data rule. Compare with an actual known optical/condensed-matter string/wave observable only with sourced uncertainty and parameters.

This correction preserves the demonstrated formal high-water mark rather than upgrading or dismissing it.

New intake, 1 November 2026: EQ-0006
Issue #14 records a conditional norm theorem for the historical SAT.O13 display M_l = m0 U_T^k. Source date 2025-06-20; versioning blob 467a0c6a24a6f4f071fc20af438420f69b6abe61; available PDF text witness blob d50427f21539ca983bc0a1344dc13aa24936c6b5 (standalone O13 module not recovered).

HsH PR #1220 is draft/open/unmerged. Its new module blob 29115ccc65df0ae671f37b06ca80a83e29998e1b and root import blob 17eca2f0b31689f118422b49fac1ff88a07456b6 match the PR head and the successful merge-reference build. Workflow run #41330715937, job 124920032346, used Lean 4.34.0 / Lake 5.0.0 and completed 8,945 build jobs. The dedicated axiom-audit step was disabled/skipped; #print axioms reports standard dependencies including propext, Classical.choice, and Quot.sound; no sorryAx appeared in the retrieved build log.

Scope: the scalar-times-isometry ansatz scales every vector norm equally. This does not formalize the separate loop-dressing term, prove a physical mass hierarchy, establish the A4-to-three-flavor projection, or validate physical premises. Status remains INTAKE / MATHEMATICALLY CHECKED / NOT AN H(s)H CANDIDATE / PHYSICALLY UNVALIDATED. Workshop line-by-line review is required before any candidacy consideration.

# FACTORY FLOOR / FLOORROOM — TO-DO

**Repository:** Satobloc/FACTORY_FLOOR  
**Established:** 2026-10-02  
**Status:** ACTIVE WORKING CONTROL SURFACE  
**Authority:** workflow / presentation / circulation only. This file does not define SAT>H(s)H theory truth.

## Floor topology

The repository README defines the Factory Floor sequence as:

**NEEDS + FIXES -> 1 GLASS+HSH -> 2 WORKSHOP -> 3 LIVE -> 4 ARTIFACTS -> 5 WEBSITE -> [[NATHAN]]**

This file turns that sequence into an explicit task graph.

The working interpretation is:

- **NEEDS + FIXES** = defects, missing pieces, stale surfaces, broken routes, unprocessed intake.
- **1 GLASS+HSH** = material arriving from the Glass Sausage Factory / SAT>H(s)H research ecosystem.
- **2 WORKSHOP** = workbench where items are repaired, derived, formatted, classified, indexed, or rendered.
- **3 LIVE** = current active stream / Floorroom circulation. Useful now, visibly status-labeled, not automatically canonical.
- **4 ARTIFACTS** = durable outputs: papers, PDFs, figures, datasets, solvers, indexes, conversation exports, audio, etc.
- **5 WEBSITE** = public presentation layer, after provenance and presentation gating.
- **[[NATHAN]]** = owner review, decision, approval, or redirect where human judgment is required.

## Global rules

1. **One object, one identity.** Each item gets a stable Floor ID and keeps source pointers as it moves.
2. **Do not confuse circulation with authority.** LIVE means current/visible, not canonical theory.
3. **Never lose provenance.** Preserve source repo/path, commit/blob identity when practical, originator, date, and status.
4. **Presentation is not source.** Human-facing derivatives can repair formatting but must not silently alter scientific content.
5. **No raw backend junk as reader content.** JSON, Markdown source, TeX source, scripts, packet files, workflow files, and control manifests are not default public reading pages.
6. **Visual authority:** P = precision/script-drawn; H = controlled hybrid downstream of exact work; I = illustrative. Class I is not geometry evidence.
7. **SAT archive is historical quarry.** H(s)H current work must distinguish historical source, current reconstruction, sandbox conjecture, and checked result.
8. **PRIOR_ART stays soft-quarantined.** External literature may constrain/compare; it is not endogenous H(s)H authority.
9. **Factory-floor/tools licensing remains a separate approval lane.** Do not let a site deploy imply licensing approval.
10. **No silent drops.** Unsupported/unroutable items go to NEEDS + FIXES with a reason.

---

# NEEDS + FIXES

### FF-001 — Build the authoritative Floor task graph
**Priority:** P0  
**Status:** STARTED  
Create a machine-readable companion to this file with:
- Floor ID
- title
- stage
- status
- priority
- owner
- source(s)
- dependencies
- blocker
- acceptance test
- destination(s)
- updated timestamp

**Acceptance:** “What still needs doing?” can be answered from Factory Floor without reconstructing state from chat history.

### FF-002 — Define one item/packet state contract
**Priority:** P0  
**Status:** NEXT  
Unify the useful fields already spread across HsH live-influx packets, Current Work feeds, paper feeds, gallery feeds, staging manifests, and site routing controls.

Minimum state dimensions:
- theory status: current / sandbox / historical / quarantined / superseded
- workflow status: candidate / queued / in-workshop / ready / incorporated / parked / blocked
- presentation status: PRESENTED / PRESENTATION PENDING / SOURCE ONLY / CACHED / OFFLINE
- visibility: private / internal / public-candidate / public
- visual class where relevant: P / H / I
- source identity
- presentation derivative identity
- related / supersedes / superseded-by

**Acceptance:** the same item can move 1 -> 2 -> 3 -> 4 -> 5 without being re-described ad hoc at every stage.

### FF-003 — Inventory broken public routes / raw-fallback regressions
**Priority:** P0  
**Status:** OPEN  
Known class: machine/control objects being rendered as ordinary reader documents; raw Markdown/TeX manuscript presentation; unresolved figure dependencies.

**Acceptance:** every known regression has a Floor ID, owner, test URL/path, and resolved state.

# 2 — WORKSHOP

### FF-203 — Geometry/visual rendering workshop

- NOW / date strip

- archive/reconstruction progress
- solver/tests
- infrastructure/site changes
- blocked / needs-Nathan items
- stale/cached/offline state

**Acceptance:** someone opening Floorroom can tell what happened today, what is active, what changed, and what is blocked.

**Priority:** P0  
**Status:** PARTIAL  
Curated/current items and candidate worker submissions must be visibly different.

**Acceptance:** a candidate packet cannot appear to be accepted theory merely because it is live.

## Formal-verification intake desk | 26 October 2026

**This is an intake register, not a completed scientific showroom.** The issue descriptions below document bounded Lean mathematical checks and original-source evidence. They do not by themselves establish H(s)H physical models, new physics, measured predictions, theorem-scope completeness, or LIVE/WEBSITE publication. Preserve the original Factory Floor chain: **1 GLASS+HSH → 2 WORKSHOP → 3 LIVE → 4 ARTIFACTS → 5 WEBSITE**.

| Entry | Source / checked mathematical content | Lean CI readback | Current Factory state |
|---|---|---|---|
| [EQ-0001 / issue #2](https://github.com/Satobloc/FACTORY_FLOOR/issues/2) | Three equal sphere carrier radius identities and selected algebraic endpoint/inequality statements; **embedded regular-circle topology not fully proved in Lean** | [run 39987817298](https://github.com/Satobloc/HsH/actions/runs/39987817298): `verify` success | INTAKE / eligible for candidacy consideration only |
| [EQ-0002 / issue #3](https://github.com/Satobloc/FACTORY_FLOOR/issues/3) | Isovolumetric deformation exponential/trace identities; **constitutive dynamics not derived** | [run 39996929325](https://github.com/Satobloc/HsH/actions/runs/39996929325): `verify` success | INTAKE / mathematically checked |
| [EQ-0003 / issue #1](https://github.com/Satobloc/FACTORY_FLOOR/issues/1) | Historical filament dispersion equation **algebraic rearrangement only**, not field-action proof | [run 39960043562](https://github.com/Satobloc/HsH/actions/runs/39960043562): `verify` success | INTAKE / theory unvalidated |
| [EQ-0004 / issue #4](https://github.com/Satobloc/FACTORY_FLOOR/issues/4) | Finite-dimensional/action field Hamiltonian Legendre identities; **not a selected H(s)H action** | [run 40005392966](https://github.com/Satobloc/HsH/actions/runs/40005392966): `verify` success | INTAKE / theory unvalidated |
| [EQ-0005 / issue #5](https://github.com/Satobloc/FACTORY_FLOOR/issues/5) | Classical linear-wave/action and ring-mode algebra; **no H(s)H finite-core constitutive coefficients or held-out observable derived** | [run 40040647137](https://github.com/Satobloc/HsH/actions/runs/40040647137): `verify` success | INTAKE / theory unvalidated |
| [EQ-0006 / issue #14](https://github.com/Satobloc/FACTORY_FLOOR/issues/14) | SAT.O13 scalar-times-unitary norm gate; **loop-dressed mass matrix and A4-to-three-flavor projection not formalized** | [run 41330715937](https://github.com/Satobloc/HsH/actions/runs/41330715937): success, 8,945 jobs | INTAKE / algebraic check only; not an H(s)H candidate |

At the 26 October inspection, the shared [HsH formal-verification pull request #262](https://github.com/Satobloc/HsH/pull/262) reported `open`, `merged=false`, `mergeable=false`. The individual verified issue reports identify original SAT archive source paths and committed proof variants. GitHub Actions job `verify` status was independently fetched for all five runs then cited. That constitutes **CI outcome readback**, not line-by-line proof-body adjudication; do that in the WORKSHOP.

**Before a showroom/public-facing promotion:** independently open every exact Lean proof file and matching historical raw-source context; verify inputs, hypotheses, theorem statement, no hidden axioms/`sorry`, toolchain and reproducibility, external physical-data status, source/candidate separation, and editorial/presentation routing. Retain false-starts and negative gates. A finished showroom should link exact proof SHAs and explain explicitly what the theorem did *not* establish.

**Current project-return route:** [HsH Phase Steering, S04](https://github.com/Satobloc/HsH/blob/main/WORKSPACES/COMMON/PHASE_STEERING_2026-10-26.md). This is a front-door index over existing issues, not an independent assertion of theorem validity.


### Follow-up direct proof-source reading, 26 October 2026

Read all five exact source proof files from [HsH branch `infra/lean4-verification-20261023`](https://github.com/Satobloc/HsH/tree/infra/lean4-verification-20261023/FORMAL_VERIFICATION/HsHVerification):
`ArchiveEQ0001.lean` blob `12f52f9530e3d73a23f33aac99c775f2df236e91`; `ArchiveEQ0002.lean` blob `f98ef92a5f62b60818d8d78f952f23f5d2e4d8fa`; `ArchiveEQ0003.lean` blob `e126892ff8912771c7aee5da0cdd0d64f5d2fc80`; `ArchiveEQ0004.lean` blob `1726467eefab4ca44cfb7dd366069ee02660bcd3`; `ArchiveEQ0005.lean` blob `142e8ff5a037a4508b581bf146913f070e1f894d`.

**Scope audit:** All five fetched Lean sources contain theorem proofs using library tactics rather than `sorry`, new `axiom` declarations or `admit`. This is **source-code inspection** coupled to previously verified CI run outcomes; it does not establish that *every* theorem in imported Mathlib or historical source is checked here. The actual theorem statements make their needed input equations explicit as hypotheses. `EQ-0001` proves real-number radius relations, not existence of embedded intersection topology; `EQ-0002` proves real exponential identities, not finite-strain dynamics; `EQ-0003` rearranges assumed dispersion; `EQ-0004` performs finite-dimensional Legendre identities; `EQ-0005` proves assumed local balance algebra and dispersion/zero-mode identities, not Euler–Lagrange derivation, independently physical coefficients or observed data.

**First historical-source follow-up:** inspected [SAT archive `SAT O Derivations/CODE-LOCKED DERIVS.txt`](https://github.com/Satobloc/SAT_THEORY_ARCHIVE_2023-25/blob/main/SAT%20O%20Derivations/CODE-LOCKED%20DERIVS.txt) D2, lines 21–114, source blob `783b05d39d59bf854b92090e15d205e6823b51c8`. The source writes periodic `k_n=2πn/L` and `omega_n=|k_n|sqrt(T/m)`, then includes factors `1/sqrt(2*m*omega_n)` in the field-operator expansion **without an explicit `n≠0` restriction in that displayed sum**. Its `n=0` mode makes the denominator zero. Standard mathematical resolution requires excluding the zero mode from those oscillator sums or treating the constant/center-of-mass mode separately. **Mark D2 quantum-mode expansion `REQUIRES_ZERO_MODE_TREATMENT` pending full intended physical/quantum source check**, not `Lean disproved` or `physically falsified`. Related issue #5 already proves `mu≠0, mu*omega²=0 ⇒ omega=0`, but it does **not** formalize the source's singular `1/sqrt(omega)` coefficient or correct it. Distinguish `m` and linear-density `mu` as noted in existing intake.

**Next WORKSHOP test:** isolate precisely the physical 1D density and the zero-mode Hilbert-space construction; verify source derivational obligations around mode normalization and canonical commutation relations, without invented physical measurements. Any new physical calculation must meet current real-system observational-data rule. Compare with an actual known optical/condensed-matter string/wave observable only with sourced uncertainty and parameters.

This correction preserves the demonstrated formal high-water mark rather than upgrading or dismissing it.


### New intake, 1 November 2026: EQ-0006

Issue [#14](https://github.com/Satobloc/FACTORY_FLOOR/issues/14) records a conditional norm theorem for the historical SAT.O13 display M_l = m0 U_T^k. Source date 2025-06-20; versioning blob 467a0c6a24a6f4f071fc20af438420f69b6abe61; available PDF text witness blob d50427f21539ca983bc0a1344dc13aa24936c6b5 (standalone O13 module not recovered).

HsH PR [#1220](https://github.com/Satobloc/HsH/pull/1220) is draft/open/unmerged. Its new module blob 29115ccc65df0ae671f37b06ca80a83e29998e1b and root import blob 17eca2f0b31689f118422b49fac1ff88a07456b6 match the PR head and the successful merge-reference build. Workflow run [#41330715937](https://github.com/Satobloc/HsH/actions/runs/41330715937), job 124920032346, used Lean 4.34.0 / Lake 5.0.0 and completed 8,945 build jobs. The dedicated axiom-audit step was disabled/skipped; #print axioms reports standard dependencies including propext, Classical.choice, and Quot.sound; no sorryAx appeared in the retrieved build log.

**Scope:** the scalar-times-isometry ansatz scales every vector norm equally. This does not formalize the separate loop-dressing term, prove a physical mass hierarchy, establish the A4-to-three-flavor projection, or validate physical premises. Status remains **INTAKE / MATHEMATICALLY CHECKED / NOT AN H(s)H CANDIDATE / PHYSICALLY UNVALIDATED**. Workshop line-by-line review is required before any candidacy consideration.

# Factory Floor Item State Contract (proposal)

**Status:** PROPOSAL / NON-OPERATIVE UNTIL REVIEWED  
**Purpose:** implement FF-002 without replacing existing source or equation registries.

## Design decision

Use a **thin lifecycle envelope** around each canonical research artifact. The envelope tracks where an item is in the wider research system; the source repository remains the home of the actual equation, proof, calculation, paper, image, or conversation.

This avoids creating a competing equation database. In particular:

- HsH `formalization/schema/equation.schema.json` remains the equation-specific registry schema.
- HsH `WORKSPACES/COMMON/EQUATION_INTAKE_0001_LANE_REGISTER_2026-10-23.md` remains a source/equation intake record.
- HsH `BEDROCK.md` remains theory-premise authority.
- Factory Floor owns cross-repository workflow state and the process record, not theory truth.
- Existing IDs such as `EQ-0001` should be retained; the envelope points to canonical source records rather than renumbering them.

## Independent status dimensions

Do not collapse these into one status field:

| Dimension | Question answered | Example |
|---|---|---|
| `theory_status` | What is this item's relationship to current H(s)H? | `sandbox` |
| `workflow_status` | Where is it in the intake/review/promotion process? | `ci_passed` |
| `math_status` | What mathematical work has actually been completed? | `lean_checked` |
| `physical_status` | What empirical standing does it have? | `no_prediction` |
| `presentation_status` | Is there an approved reader-facing derivative? | `source_only` |
| `visibility` | Who may see it? | `internal` |

A single object may validly be `theory_status=sandbox`, `math_status=lean_checked`, `physical_status=no_prediction`. That is not a contradiction.

## Provenance and authorship

Each source reference records repository, path, source role and, when available, commit/blob SHA, locator, author/speaker and date. Distinguish Nathan Direct, historical author, assistant-generated reconstruction, external source, mixed authorship and unresolved attribution.

Do not infer Nathan's intent from a generated summary. Apply the HsH Original-Statement-First Research Gate before substantive theory work.

## Promotion semantics

- `ci_passed` means the recorded CI job succeeded. It is not mathematical clearance.
- `workshop_cleared` is reserved for a bounded mathematical review of source fidelity, assumptions, derivation, proof, dimensions, signs, limits, topology and reproducibility.
- `eligible_for_candidacy_consideration` is the maximum implication of Factory Floor mathematical clearance. It is not an H(s)H candidate.
- `incorporated` requires an explicit Nathan-authorized construction decision and a versioned HsH destination.
- Physical assessment is an independent axis. No previous state implies physical validation.

## BEDROCK constraints

- Real observational/experimental systems and data are the default for physical calculations.
- Data-free calculations, including pure-geometry and numerical-fixture calculations, require Nathan's explicit prior permission.
- Preserve historical source equations and corrections; do not silently normalize or overwrite conflicting versions.
- A failed or unresolved result remains a durable record, not a deleted row.

## Schema

The companion JSON Schema is `schemas/floor_item.schema.json`. It validates the envelope only. It intentionally does not attempt to validate LaTeX, Lean proof correctness, physical data, or theory adoption.

## Migration plan

1. Review this contract and schema.
2. Map existing Factory Floor EQ records to envelopes without changing their current statuses.
3. Link to existing HsH equation entries and Lean source/CI artifacts; do not duplicate equation bodies.
4. Add non-equation objects only after the ID namespace and ownership are agreed.
5. Validate records and check referential integrity before enabling automation.
6. Keep all migration changes reversible and source-linked.

## Not yet done

No existing item has been migrated, no status has been changed, and no automation consumes this schema. This proposal is not an adoption decision.

README.md
Story Morph v0.2
Second-round prototype: the engine now distinguishes world truth, character/reader belief, event order, typed relations, multi-input dependencies, reinterpretation events, and latent repair buds.

Key change
A revelation can now change the meaning of prior material without changing the historical facts. Reinterpretation records that operation explicitly.

The Nellibeth model includes three braided lanes:

material/systemic
relational/epistemic
wound/ethical
Reproducibility
Structural IDs, state IDs, run IDs, and record hashes are content-derived. Seeded mutation selection records the seed, candidate mutation IDs, and selected mutation. Wall-clock time is deliberately excluded from canonical records.

Run
python demo.py
Seed 27 chooses one of four candidate mutations. Change the seed or candidates and compare the archived run.

Current limits
This is a source-grounded micro-model, not yet a sentence/beat-level exhaustive encoding. Chronology and reveal order are represented, but temporal interval logic, prose-generation, automatic repair, branch manifests, and source-span provenance remain future work.



Satobloc
HsH

README.md
Story Morph v0.2
Second-round prototype: the engine now distinguishes world truth, character/reader belief, event order, typed relations, multi-input dependencies, reinterpretation events, and latent repair buds.

Key change
A revelation can now change the meaning of prior material without changing the historical facts. Reinterpretation records that operation explicitly.

The Nellibeth model includes three braided lanes:

material/systemic
relational/epistemic
wound/ethical
Reproducibility
Structural IDs, state IDs, run IDs, and record hashes are content-derived. Seeded mutation selection records the seed, candidate mutation IDs, and selected mutation. Wall-clock time is deliberately excluded from canonical records.

Run
python demo.py
Seed 27 chooses one of four candidate mutations. Change the seed or candidates and compare the archived run.

Current limits
This is a source-grounded micro-model, not yet a sentence/beat-level exhaustive encoding. Chronology and reveal order are represented, but temporal interval logic, prose-generation, automatic repair, branch manifests, and source-span provenance remain future work.



Satobloc
HsH

# SAT/H(s)H Archive Tools — Roaming Card

Use the broadest useful archive surface. Do not assume the current repo or current vocabulary contains the thing you are looking for.

## Search / retrieval

- **Mersearch / Mercer_Searcher** — Primary corpus-wide body-search route. Use for terms, concepts, names, precursors, terminology drift, and deliberately divergent searches. Search variants and neighboring concepts, not just exact modern vocabulary.
- **Conversation Viewer / manifests** — Wayfinding across exported conversations: dates, titles, canonical raw paths, archive location, and direct conversation inspection. The manifest is navigation metadata, not proof that a term is absent from conversation bodies.
- **Convos archives — including `convos_21` and earlier/later tranches** — Raw conversation history. Search across all available archive generations when reconstructing an idea, person/instance, terminology, chronology, or provenance. Very large/zero-byte-looking entries may actually mean **really big**, not empty.
- **SAT/H(s)H repos and GitHub search/history** — Search current files, historical files, commits, diffs, issues, handoffs, Labs, Commons, worker outputs, source indexes, and deleted/superseded states when recoverable. Commit history is often essential for chronology.
- **HSH_RESOURCES** — Project research/source collection, including historical project materials and outside resources. Use exact provenance and authorship/status labels.
- **NLM / numbered archive folders** — Deep source-recovery layer. Folder numbers are wayfinding, not authority or chronology. Follow indexes back to originals where possible and flag gaps.
- **Nathan Words / Nathan-source excavation** — Use for Nathan’s actual language, conceptual ancestry, reversals, terminology, methodological statements, and voice. A Nathan-source item is evidence of what was said in that context, not automatic current-theory authority.
- **Braintrust Memorial Commons / Field Notes / Labs / Q&A / handoffs / check-ins** — Useful for hypotheses, failed approaches, recommendations, unresolved questions, cross-instance observations, and pointers into deeper sources. Treat as working records, not established theory.
- **Revival materials / historical-instance records** — Use to recover earlier instances, methods, forgotten results, first-blush reactions, and historical terminology. Search aliases: e.g. Alberr / Albert / Einstein and other known identity variants.
- **Public-site / Glass Sausage Factory feeds and artifacts** — Useful for locating presentation-ready material, public chronology, papers, conversations, quotes/news, and publication state. Public presentation does not by itself establish theory status.

## Search behavior

Search conceptually and historically, not only lexically. Try modern term → older synonyms → descriptive phrases → opposite framing → nearby dates → likely conversations → original artifact.

When useful, deliberately search somewhere unlikely: old SAT, philosophy/methodology, abandoned branches, unrelated-looking conversations, historical instances, or material opposite your normal specialty. Unexpected ancestry is often valuable.

Prefer the original source over an index, summary, recollection, or later retelling. Preserve exact path/date/author/instance/status when something matters.

Absence from one index, viewer, repo, folder, or keyword search is **not evidence of absence from the archives**.

## Quarantine / sandbox — hard rule

**PRIOR_ART, explicitly private material, quarantined repos/folders, and any source marked QUARANTINE are closed unless your current assignment explicitly grants access.** Do not search into them “just to check.” Do not leak their vocabulary, targets, solution methods, citations, or conclusions into unexposed workers or internal theory development.

If you are an authorized quarantined observer, return only through the designated quarantine-safe interface and preserve the requested blinding/de-dup/provenance boundary.

**SANDBOXED material may be inspected when otherwise accessible, but sandbox ≠ established theory, validation, publication, or authority.** Watercooler, Field Notes, Labs, Nathan-source material, revival material, worker speculation, and sandbox papers likewise remain status-typed.

When uncertain: **preserve the boundary, record the pointer, and route the question rather than crossing it.**

## Default output from an archive roam

Bring back the useful thing itself, plus:

**where it came from → when → who/what produced it → exact status → why it matters → what it does not establish → best next pointer.**

Do not silently modernize historical terminology or back-project current H(s)H concepts into older SAT material.

README.md
H(s)H Workspaces
WORKSPACES/ is the noncanonical working layer for focused agents, experiments, reconstruction passes, audits-in-progress, and other substantial tasks that need room to develop before their results are promoted into the repository's durable layers.

A workspace is a workbench, not an authority source. It may contain unfinished reasoning, competing approaches, temporary notation, negative results, or abandoned branches. Source authority remains with the original project records and external sources; current theory status remains with the appropriate synthesis/ledger/checkpoint surfaces.

When and how to make a personal workspace
Create or request a personal workspace when a scoped task would materially benefit from private-to-the-lane working memory: notes, to-do lists, source cursors, intermediate decisions, local conventions, capability reminders, open questions, handoff state, or continuity material that is useful to that instance/task but is not clearly useful enough for Commons-, repository-, function-, or project-wide notification.

Do not use a personal workspace to hide consequential shared state. Anything another lane needs to know, anything that changes project-wide procedure or theory status, and anything durable enough to become repository infrastructure should still be surfaced in the appropriate Common or canonical layer.

When a personal workspace would help:

Tell Nathan. Do not silently create a durable instance identity if Nathan has not seen it.
Choose a stable name if you do not already have one. If you already have a name but there may be divergent, partially derived, resumed, or ambiguously related instances, add a surname or other stable discriminator rather than assuming identity continuity.
Attach the originating conversation provenance. Record the conversation ID and, when available, the relevant raw message/node ID and timestamp. If the live conversation has not yet entered the archive, use an explicit pending-backfill marker rather than inventing identifiers.
Create the workspace under WORKSPACES/<NAME>/ unless a function-specific workspace is clearly more appropriate. Keep its README.md concise: identity/discriminator, role, scoped objective, conversation provenance, boundaries, current state, and pointers to any durable outputs.
Add or update the instance placard in Common. The placard is a short directory-facing record: name/discriminator, role, principal capabilities, current lane, important documents/context actually possessed or loaded, major constraints, workspace path, and source conversation pointer. It is not a full check-in or biography.
Keep local state local until it matters elsewhere. Promote only what becomes a shared dependency, durable result, reusable tool, important warning, or handoff.
Before a long conversation approaches failure/cutoff, write continuity state. Record the current objective, live TODOs, key working assumptions/conventions, exact artifacts/paths, unresolved questions, and the smallest useful restart packet for a successor or resumed instance.
The point is not to manufacture personas. The point is to reduce information loss when long conversations end abruptly and to preserve enough of a productive scoped working configuration that it can be inspected, resumed, compared, or partially reconstructed later.

See WORKSPACES/COMMON/INSTANCE_PLACARDS.md and WORKSPACES/COMMON/CONTINUITY_PROTOCOL.md.

Recommended workspace pattern
A sustained workspace may use whichever files its job requires, but these names are preferred when useful:

README.md — scope, role, boundaries, identity/provenance, and current objective.
TODO.md — actionable queue.
RECORD.md — concise chronological work log and decisions.
SOURCES.md — source inputs and exact provenance pointers.
CROSSWALK.md — links between workspace results and source/current destinations.
CONTINUITY.md — restart packet / current working state when a long-running instance would be costly to lose.
Do not manufacture empty files merely to satisfy the pattern.

Common coordination area
WORKSPACES/COMMON/ is the shared coordination surface for work that crosses individual workspaces or agents. It is for concise handoffs, blockers, shared questions, notices of useful discoveries, and coordination state. It should not become a second synthesis or a miscellaneous dump.

Use:

WORKSPACES/COMMON/COORDINATION.md for active shared state and questions.
WORKSPACES/COMMON/HANDOFFS.md for concise handoffs between workers/workspaces.
WORKSPACES/COMMON/INSTANCE_PLACARDS.md for short identity/role/capability/workspace cards.
WORKSPACES/COMMON/CONTINUITY_PROTOCOL.md for continuity/reconstruction guidance.
When an item becomes durable, move or restate the result in the proper repository layer and leave only a pointer in the common area.

Repository-boundary rule
Public source/provenance cross-linking is primarily between this repository and Satobloc/SAT_THEORY_ARCHIVE_2023-25.

Satobloc/HSH_RESOURCES is private and reference-only. Do not make a public workspace depend on a private HSH_RESOURCES GitHub link. When external material found there matters, use the original bibliographic identifier/source and Chicago-style citation, or carry forward an attributed quotation/extract or summary/paraphrase with sufficient provenance. Internal/private research may retain the exact resource path/hash separately for retrieval.

The historical archive has its own archive-side common surface at .[⚙️_AI_FILES]/SHARED_RESOURCES/; this WORKSPACES/COMMON/ area is the current H(s)H build-side coordination surface.



# Onboarding Pointer

New, newer, reassigned, or revived instances should begin with:

- `WORKSPACES/COMMON/NEW_INSTANCE_START_HERE.md`
- `WORKSPACES/COMMON/INSTANCE_ONBOARDING_3REPO.md`
- `WORKSPACES/COMMON/ONBOARDING_REVIEW_STATE.md`

These documents explain the distinct roles and navigation/tooling conventions of `SAT_THEORY_ARCHIVE_2023-25`, `HsH`, and `HSH_RESOURCES`, plus current shared workflow expectations.

# Instance Onboarding Review State

**2026-10-25 MATERIAL CHANGE — archive expectation audit:** The [Field Notes Assumption Revision Index](FIELD_NOTES/ASSUMPTION_REVISION_INDEX.md) now routes worker-attributed surprises and prior-expectation corrections into existing Field Notes and source indexes. The worker cold-start check must test comprehension, not link recognition: can a worker identify specific SAT-O and Blockwave high-water-mark source families, distinguish Nathan Direct from assistant interpretation, separate worked math from independently verified results, explain historic LLM roles without generational condescension, use Mersearch and dedup indexes, and cite original source context? Errors belong in a source-grounded entry; successful link clicks alone do not count as onboarding.


**2026-10-10 material change:** Added federated Cross-Formalism Index navigation to NEW_INSTANCE_START_HERE.md, INSTANCE_ONBOARDING_3REPO.md, and REFERENCE_DESK/README.md. Workers doing mathematics/solver work should absorb at next onboarding review; this is a new navigation layer, not a change to symbol authority or quarantine.


**Status:** CLEAN COLD-START PASS RECORDED / MONITOR  
**Current as of:** 2026-09-21

Purpose: track whether the central onboarding material remains accurate enough for new, newer, reassigned, and revived instances to navigate all three SAT/H(s)H repositories and their tools without relying on oral tradition.

## Current material-change flag

`CARPE_TURNEM_POLICY.md` was added on 2026-09-20 and integrated into the onboarding path. This is a material onboarding change. Established workers should absorb it at their next appropriate control/onboarding review; after absorption it does not require full rereading every recurrence.

A 2026-09-21 cold-start review found and repaired a stale orientation pointer in `INSTANCE_ONBOARDING_3REPO.md`: the guide had still routed fresh instances to historical `CURRENT_WORKFLOW_ORIENTATION.md` in two places even though `NEW_INSTANCE_START_HERE.md` correctly designated `CURRENT_WORKFLOW_ORIENTATION_V2.md` as current. The three-repository guide now points to V2 and explicitly labels the unversioned file historical. Treat that repair as absorbed once an instance has read the 2026-09-21 guide; it does not itself require repeated full-package rereads.

A second 2026-09-21 cold-start pass found that `NEW_INSTANCE_START_HERE.md` told a fresh worker to inspect the current task/branch, directive, milestone, check-in, handoff, and lease state without naming the live files. That ambiguity is now repaired: the start-here surface explicitly names the live control-plane files while retaining the instruction to read only the portions needed for the immediate bounded task.

A third 2026-09-21 cold-start pass found a scope/authority ambiguity in the new pointer block: `NATHAN_DIRECT_WORKFLOW_STATE.md` had been presented as if it supplied generic project-wide phase/milestone state, although it is specifically the Nathan Direct corpus/provenance lane state. `NEW_INSTANCE_START_HERE.md` now routes project-wide purpose/phase through `CURRENT_WORKFLOW_ORIENTATION_V2.md`, active-edge/milestone routing through `TASK_BRANCH_GRAPH.json`, directive authority through the current provenance/Common surfaces, and `NATHAN_DIRECT_WORKFLOW_STATE.md` only when the selected work actually enters that lane. This repair is also QA history, not a cold-start certification.

## Required onboarding sources

Primary guide: `WORKSPACES/COMMON/INSTANCE_ONBOARDING_3REPO.md`  
Execution rule: `WORKSPACES/COMMON/CARPE_TURNEM_POLICY.md`

The guide must cover, and periodically re-check:

- shared project workflow and current-state entry points;
- Carpe Turnem execution discipline: act on already-authorized safe next steps in the current turn rather than ending on empty agreement;
- `Satobloc/SAT_THEORY_ARCHIVE_2023-25` purpose, front doors, Dashboard/AI-files orientation, archive indices, admin/tooling conventions;
- `Satobloc/HsH` purpose, Common/current-state surfaces, conversation/viewer/index tooling, live workflow conventions;
- `Satobloc/HSH_RESOURCES` purpose, permitted resource/index/extraction/bibliography surfaces, exposure controls, and strict `PRIOR_ART` boundary;
- cross-repository source identity and routing rules;
- tool discovery: inspect existing tools before inventing replacements;
- current task/branch, handoff, check-in, revival, and scheduler/instance-pool conventions.

## Who must review it

Review is required when an instance is:

1. new to the project;
2. newly connected to the current workflow after substantial project evolution;
3. revived from an older historical instance;
4. reassigned into a repository/tooling environment it has not used recently;
5. showing navigation/tool-use errors that suggest stale orientation;
6. active when a material onboarding change is flagged and has not yet absorbed it.

Routine established workers do not need to reread the entire guide every recurrence. They should reread only when one of the triggers above applies or the guide has materially changed.

## Completion test

An instance is adequately onboarded when it can locate and explain:

- current central purpose/phase;
- current task/branch state;
- current directive and milestone state;
- where Common coordination/check-ins/handoffs live;
- which of the three repositories is authoritative for the material/task at hand;
- the repository-specific front doors and relevant tools;
- quarantine/exposure constraints;
- how to leave a durable checkpoint/return route;
- how to discover existing tools before creating new infrastructure;
- when Carpe Turnem requires immediate safe execution versus when a blocker/decision/freeze legitimately prevents it.

## Cold-start validation state

The 2026-09-21 cold-start validation first produced three real navigation defects:

1. `INSTANCE_ONBOARDING_3REPO.md` disagreed with `NEW_INSTANCE_START_HERE.md` about which workflow-orientation document was current. Repaired.
2. `NEW_INSTANCE_START_HERE.md` named categories of live control state without naming their actual control surfaces, forcing a new worker to infer which similarly named Common files were current. Repaired by adding explicit live-control-plane pointers.
3. The resulting pointer block over-scoped `NATHAN_DIRECT_WORKFLOW_STATE.md` as though it were generic project-wide phase/milestone authority. Repaired by separating project-wide orientation/task-graph routing from Nathan Direct lane state.

### First clean cold-start pass — 2026-09-21

A fresh validation starting only from `NEW_INSTANCE_START_HERE.md` and the surfaces it explicitly routes to recovered all seven required items without oral correction or guessed repository paths:

1. **Live central task/branch state:** `TASK_BRANCH_GRAPH.json` is explicitly named by Start Here and exposes branch status, dependencies, active edge, next cursor, and return route.
2. **Current directive and milestone state:** Start Here separates project-wide phase (`CURRENT_WORKFLOW_ORIENTATION_V2.md`), active-edge/milestone routing (`TASK_BRANCH_GRAPH.json`), and directive authority/provenance (`NATHAN_DIRECTIVES_PROVENANCE.md` plus newer explicitly controlling Common artifacts). It no longer misuses the Nathan Direct lane state as generic project authority.
3. **Common check-in/handoff surfaces:** `CHECKINS.md` and `HANDOFFS.md` are explicitly named; the orientation also routes routine questions through Common/Q&A surfaces.
4. **Active/paused execution-lease state:** `ACTIVE_AUTOMATION_ROSTER.md` and `INSTANCE_REGISTRY_EXECUTION_LEASES.md` are explicitly named and correctly distinguish scheduled leases from paused/unscheduled/historical instance identity.
5. **Three repository roles/front doors:** `INSTANCE_ONBOARDING_3REPO.md` gives distinct purposes, start-here surfaces, operational conventions, and cross-repository routing for `SAT_THEORY_ARCHIVE_2023-25`, `HsH`, and `HSH_RESOURCES`.
6. **Quarantine/exposure rules:** the onboarding package explicitly preserves strict `PRIOR_ART`/private quarantine, exposure history, source-role separation, and repository-specific evidentiary roles.
7. **One executable bounded task:** the live task graph itself exposes `CTRL-05` as the bounded cold-start validation operation with an explicit completion test; the current prompt/lease also authorizes taking the highest-value bounded operation immediately.

**Result:** the onboarding package now satisfies the `CTRL-05` cold-start exit criterion on this pass. No fourth documentation defect was found. Do not manufacture additional onboarding edits merely to keep the branch active.

**Routing disposition:** `CTRL-05` is ready to be marked completed in `TASK_BRANCH_GRAPH.json`; once that graph reconciliation is made, parent `CTRL-2026-09-20-GENERALIST` may also be evaluated against its exit criterion because cold-start QA was its remaining stated dependency. This file records the QA result; the task graph remains the routing authority.

**Next cursor:** reconcile the task graph to this clean-pass result, then return execution capacity to the highest-value substantive active edge. Re-open onboarding QA only on a material-change trigger or a concrete navigation/tooling failure.

## Maintenance trigger

Any worker who encounters a stale path, missing tool explanation, changed repository role, confusing front door, undocumented workflow, or recurring empty-agreement behavior should open/update an onboarding-documentation or workflow task rather than silently working around the defect.

The Comptroller/generalist workflow should periodically choose a bounded onboarding-review operation when:

- major workflow/milestone changes occur;
- repository control surfaces change;
- new tooling is introduced;
- revived/new instances repeatedly ask the same navigation question;
- a repo-specific orientation is discovered to be misleading or incomplete;
- workers repeatedly acknowledge settled actions without executing them despite having authority and tools.

Onboarding is infrastructure, not theory authority. Keep it concise, current, navigational, and explicit about uncertainty/status boundaries.

Use this as a bounded review queue, not a permanent specialist lane.

README.md
H(s)H Worldtube Lab
Live construction workspace for Nathan + Ravel + Janus.

Purpose
This workspace is for bounded forward-construction work on particle/worldtube geometry. Slack is the live bench; this directory is the durable state. It is not a source-authority layer by itself.

Current handoff packets
KERR_CORE_BASELINE.md — provisional Kerr/ER core geometry baseline for forward construction.
OPERATOR_NORMALIZATION_HANDOFF.md — operator grammar, ᚼ / ᚼᚼ notation, common length-coordinate normalization, residual-anisotropy audit, instrument roles, Lagrangian conversion, and worldtube entry checklist.
OPERATOR_SPEC.json — compact machine-readable state for the operator/normalization handoff.
Default work sequence
Native standard formulation / measurement.
All-purpose normalization audit (w = ct plus declared invertible maps).
Residual structure and scale ledger.
Minimal idealized worldtube geometry.
Readout / slice map.
Independent collision test or holdout.
Freeze packet.
Only then, where useful, formal audit, archive recovery, and reconciliation.
Current scientific front
Standard filament / Kerr-ER core -> genuine near-core deformable structure -> persistent coil -> finite-timesheet intersection/readout -> measurable particle-scale consequence.

Current first target: persistent-coil triangulation from genuinely independent observables, initially e/mu/tau unless a stronger minimal system presents itself.

Working assumptions under active attack
The scale a = hbar/(2mc) is assigned to the standard-filament Kerr/ER core rather than to a higher-order coil or readout scale.
Persistent coiling is the correct geometric carrier of rest-mass behavior in the model.
A suitable genuine near-core structure exists that can support the coupled force deformation represented on the filament side.
These are not promoted facts. Each should have an internal redundancy check and an external collision test.

Claim packet
Every substantive claim should record:

question / claim ID
owner
firsthand scope: read / calculated / participated / inferred / handoff
native equation or measured inputs
authorized inputs
forbidden inputs / holdouts
normalization transformations
scale ledger
residual structure
H(s)H mapping
assumptions
failure condition
freeze condition
intended handoff
Representation discipline
Keep distinct:

reduced Minkowski/worldworm representation;
full 4D worldtube/bulk representation;
actual 3D or finite-thickness timesheet intersection/readout.
A structure appearing in one representation is not automatically a primitive of another.

Geometry discipline
Simplified and idealized geometry is expected during metric discovery. Exactness is development-phase dependent, not role-owned. Solver work should recover the corresponding simplified geometry in the appropriate limit.

Promotion discipline
Nothing generated by unattended or external agents promotes itself. Freeze first; inspect dependencies; then reconcile. Archive recovery may be deliberately withheld until after freeze when semi-blind convergence has evidentiary value.

Quality target
Evaluate work against multiple bars: competent working physics, top-tier contemporary work, historically strong theoretical construction, and the stricter H(s)H internal bar of dependency purity, reproducibility, independent constraint, adversarial survival, representational consistency, and explicit failure conditions.

# Mira | 2026-10-28 11:00Z | Hinton-Smith-Nathan film/filament source fidelity

**Status:** ORIGINAL SOURCE / BIBLIOGRAPHIC DEPENDENCY VERIFIED / RIGID-TRANSLATION THEOREMS SUBMITTED / LEAN CI SEPARATE / NO PHYSICAL CLAIM. Current assistant working under Mira's historical bibliography and citation charter, not authenticated historical Mira. Run `REVROT3-19-20261011:419:s0`, next Aldus.

## Original witnesses actually inspected
- Nathan user-role original `DEVELOPMENT_FULL_CONVOS/SAT_CONVOS_11/24.03.22•26.06.01•DIMENSIONAL GRAVITY — raw.json`, Git blob `2cea1924266418e19b825b95ae51e6b7cbace7e1`: parsed all 466 mapping nodes and first eight user-role messages, first user UUID `aaa28bd9-07f7-4740-b878-b485d4db44d0`. Its creation time `1711120205.406399` resolves to **2024-03-22 15:10:05 UTC (11:10 EDT)**. The separate screenshot-derived SAT archive `Mar23.2024 - DIMENSIONAL GRAVITY - iPhone_Screenshot - bellomy.txt`, blob `339fddec01b717bedffed770f1b73f8bab103fb5`, labels March 23. Treat this as a **date-label discrepancy**, not proof the first original message occurred March 23; the screenshot may have been captured later. Export-clock integrity not independently tested.
- Charles Howard Hinton, *The Fourth Dimension*, first edition April **1904**, consulted second edition May **1906**, Gutenberg eBook #67153 (released 2022, updated 2024). Full text read/search coverage 449,282 characters / 8,870 lines; section around lines 985-1045 specifically read. A stationary spiral intersected by a moving film gives a moving point; filaments intersected by a film give apparent atoms. The same passage later considers **moving structures** as a modification. A bibliographic catalog's 1904 first-publication date and a 1906 witness are not conflicting dates of the same event.
- W. Whately Smith, *A Theory of the Mechanism of Survival: The Fourth Dimension and Its Applications*, **1920**, Gutenberg eBook #64055 (released 2020, updated 2024). Full text read/search coverage 251,753 characters / 5,282 lines; lines 295-304, 2655-2720 specifically read. Smith **explicitly cites Hinton's book, page 6**, before his later soap-film/thin-thread/complexly intertwined thread description. The later passage allows filaments to be altered and explicitly disclaims a literal picture of reality.
- Prior Mira Sept22 bibliography/continuity and Alden Cross Sept14 structural report already recognized Hinton and Smith as close historical parallels. Kestrel Oct28 09Z already checked tilted-helix ellipse and diagonal speed. Those discoveries are not re-claimed. The new contribution is dependency/edition/date typing and rigid versus deformable map.

## Finding: work-level dependency versus specific borrowing
The two older books cannot be represented as *independent work-level* origins of the dimensional analogy: Smith explicitly acknowledges Hinton. **This does not establish that Smith copied the specific later thread passage from Hinton.** A separate passage-level ancestry comparison remains open. Nathan's March22 raw conversation establishes a distinct internal source event; no pre-2024 Nathan exposure to either book was recovered here. Absence of exposure evidence is not proof of historical independence.

## Nathan Direct geometry and source translation
Nathan's raw user messages separate (a) one fixed plane intersecting a straight line and helix in exactly two points S and H, (b) a later moving plane generating a two-dimensional trace, and (c) a fixed circular orbit radius determined by transverse separation rather than helix pitch. The historical assistant initially described a static spiral and later made pitch/radius confusion; Nathan corrected both. His later tilted/diagonal constructions require additional orientation/slope conditions, previously audited by Kestrel.

Typed glossary: `C` = one-dimensional curve/filament locus in 3D analogy; `P(h)` = two-dimensional film at normal height h; `R_h(C)` = transverse coordinate section of C at h; `t -> R_{h(t)}(C)` = apparent trace, not an instantaneous section; `T_{-h}C` = rigidly translated filament; `C_t` = arbitrary time-dependent, possibly deformable filament; `worldtube` = extended finite-core 4D object, not identical to a centerline. An analogy is not a derived physical equivalence.

**Mapping diagram:**
```mermaid
flowchart LR
  H[Hinton 1904 / 1906] -->|explicit book citation p6| S[Smith 1920]
  H -->|fixed C; moving P at h| R[section R_h of C]
  S -->|moving C_t; fixed P at 0| Q[section R_0 of C_t]
  R -->|rigid relative translation only| Q
  S -->|filament deformation allowed| D[extra evolution map required]
  N[Nathan raw March22 2024] -->|instantaneous S and H then moving film| R
  R -->|finite-core promotion requires typed projection| W[4D worldtube/timesheet test hypothesis]
```

**Source-conditional mathematical identity:** for `sectionAt C h = {(x,y) | C x y h}` and `translatedFilament C h x y z = C x y (z+h)`, `sectionAt C h = sectionAt (translatedFilament C h) 0`. This only formalizes identical relative rigid translation. For arbitrary evolving D, equality requires pointwise `C x y h iff D x y 0`, not given by Smith's text. Draft [PR #885](https://github.com/Satobloc/HsH/pull/885) contains two Lean 4/Mathlib conditional theorem statements. File presence is not compilation; consult [CI run #40676865475](https://github.com/Satobloc/HsH/actions/runs/40676865475) for actual status and axiom reports. No measured physical calculation was made.

## Discrepancy ledger
1. **M11-01 VERIFIED:** Smith cites Hinton; do not claim two independent *work-level* conceptual sources. Specific later passage borrowing UNRESOLVED.
2. **M11-02 VERIFIED:** Hinton first publication 1904; consulted second-edition witness 1906.
3. **M11-03 VERIFIED:** Nathan original user timestamp March22 UTC, derivative screenshot label March23; screenshot capture date UNRESOLVED.
4. **M11-04 CONDITIONAL:** rigid film/filament relative translation yields same transverse section; Lean compilation pending actual run.
5. **M11-05 VERIFIED TEXTUAL LIMIT:** Smith permits deformation, so no universal rigid translation equivalence.
6. **M11-06 KNOWN:** Nathan corrects assistant's static-spiral and pitch/radius mistakes; do not reassign authorship.
7. **M11-07 OPEN:** 3D curve/2D film point section to 4D finite-core worldtube/3D timesheet needs explicit section/projection/composition map.
8. **M11-08 OPEN:** pre-2024 influence/exposure of Nathan to either book not established; no positive or negative causal claim.

## Paper and safety boundaries
P1 Nov9 Orson-owned cognition manuscript may use this as an example of bibliographic dependence, not an LLM experiment. P2 Nov30 still needs genuine measured physical system, units, uncertainty and held-out comparison. P3 Dec14 should cite both prior-art books and their dependency/edition status; a finished manuscript does not validate the theory. No CODATA scoring, invented physical fixture, BEDROCK promotion, unauthorized reference extraction, private repo link in public packet, task change or historical-thread contact.

Read live BEDROCK blob `4cabda482c48c217614b868a2138409275df9bf7` opening and provenance sections (not line-by-line full), Steering Charter `b9b8de55f8d5add7c42cf80be7c34677ee36305e`, Three Paper Campaign `9eb28e89a3c9b02c5b251b43f3e38cc6ec019aa0`, onboarding `7d78afd95654bd9d41bbbcc6ba56db1fd10b3110`, Revival V3/branch map and immediate Alberr 10:48 handoff blob `f18a0c227693ba15481d57a6350a5de05832df23`; Mira original specialist continuity. Recipient Aldus keeps his own source/chronology remit; repository handoff is not authenticated conversation delivery.


## 1888 primary-source deepening (later in same turn)

Full historical source Hinton, *A New Era of Thought*, London 1888, Project Gutenberg #60607, Git blob `cf0272d50e0366238fe8af9d6c60d80220ff3a94`, **401,722 characters / 8,136 lines** retrieved and searched. Lines 20-110 establish edition/title, lines **2140-2190** explicitly describe a **cube moving through a fixed plane** and the resulting square sections; then extend this to a higher-dimensional solid passing through a three-dimensional æther/section. Therefore the general dimensional moving-section construction is directly attested in Hinton **by 1888**, not first in 1904. The 1904 first edition/1906 consulted edition of *The Fourth Dimension* develops the more specific spiral, moving film and filamentary atom example. # Mira s1 | Unknown-frame SO(4) chirality discrimination
2026-10-14 20:12 UTC | Mathematical sandbox | GEN/DERIVED

## Setup and scope
Independent extension of Alberr's 20:00Z SO(4) handedness fixture. Let
`R_h=diag(R(a),R(h b))`, `h∈{+1,-1}`, `a=0.2387 rad`, `b=0.246 rad`. Observe `Y=Q R_h Qᵀ+E`, with unknown Haar-uniform `Q∈SO(4)`, and independent Gaussian entries `E_ij~N(0,σ²)`. This is a synthetic, dimensionless observation model, not a physical measurement.

## Exact invariant and sufficient test within the skew observation
Define `A=(Y-Yᵀ)/2`, and `Pf(A)=A12 A34−A13 A24+A14 A23`. For any orthogonal Q,
`Pf(QAQᵀ)=det(Q) Pf(A)`. Thus proper SO(4) frame uncertainty preserves the sign; untracked odd reflection reverses it. The noiseless values are `±sin(a)sin(b)=±0.05757929165721662`.

Write A in orthonormal self-dual and anti-self-dual coordinates:
`v_+=[(A12+A34),(A13−A24),(A14+A23)]/√2`,
`v_-=[(A12−A34),(A13+A24),(A14−A23)]/√2`.
Then `Pf(A)=(||v_+||²−||v_-||²)/2`. Each observed vector has independent isotropic Gaussian noise with variance `v=σ²/2` per coordinate. Under H+, the true vector lengths are `r_big=(sin(a)+sin(b))/√2` and `r_small=|sin(a)−sin(b)|/√2`; under H− these lengths swap. Haar SO(4) independently randomizes the two 3-vector directions.

The radial likelihood for true length r, observed length x is proportional to `exp[−(x²+r²)/(2v)] sinh(rx/v)/(rx/v)`. Its ratio at `r_big` versus `r_small` is strictly increasing with x. Therefore the exact equal-prior Bayes rule **for the skew part of Y** is `Pf(A)>0` for H+, else H−. We do **not** assert full-matrix optimality: its symmetric part may contain additional information.

Set `λ_big=r_big²/v`, `λ_small=r_small²/v`. The exact error probability is

`P_err(σ)=∫₀^∞ F_{χ'²_3(λ_big)}(t) f_{χ'²_3(λ_small)}(t) dt`.

The Pfaffian is unbiased: `E[Pf(A)]=sin(a)sin(b)`, with exact variance
`Var(Pf(A))=v[sin²(a)+sin²(b)]+3v²`.

## Executed numerical tests (NumPy/SciPy; fixed seed 202610142012)
At `σ=0.1`: exact unknown-SO4-frame skew Bayes error **0.002654648948915**; canonical 200,000-trial Pf sign error **0.002745**; random Haar SO4 70,000-trial error **0.002414286**. Expected Pf variance **0.000651044019905**; observed **0.000653356495451**. Maximum tested SO4 Pf invariance residual **8.33e−17**. Aligned-frame full-matrix Gaussian Bayes error **0.000286606361260**.

| σ | Unknown SO4 skew Bayes error | Known aligned full-matrix error |
|---:|---:|---:|
| 0.050 | 1.5436821e−10 | 2.8298050e−12 |
| 0.075 | 3.7383136e−5 | 2.1953368e−6 |
| 0.100 | 0.002654649 | 0.000286606 |
| 0.150 | 0.051828926 | 0.010838274 |
| 0.200 | 0.142366827 | 0.042535550 |
| 0.300 | 0.288286644 | 0.125485483 |

**Negative controls.** When b=0, 100,000-trial Pf sign error 0.50167; when orientation parity is unknown and equally likely odd/even, 100,000-trial error 0.49916. The latter is an **exact no-identifiability theorem**, not merely a noisy estimate: H+ and H− become identical O(4)-mixed observation distributions. Invariance under odd reflection flips Pf sign to 8.33e−17. As σ→0, risk→0; as σ→∞, risk→1/2.

## Minimal independent reproduction
```python
import numpy as np
from scipy.integrate import quad
from scipy.stats import ncx2, norm
a,b,sigma=.2387,.246,.1
v=sigma*sigma/2
lb=(np.sin(a)+np.sin(b))**2/(2*v)
ls=(np.sin(a)-np.sin(b))**2/(2*v)
err=quad(lambda t:ncx2.cdf(t,3,lb)*ncx2.pdf(t,3,ls),0,np.inf,
         epsabs=1e-12,epsrel=1e-10)[0]
aligned=norm.cdf(-np.sqrt(2)*np.sin(b)/sigma)
print(err,aligned)
```

# Mira s4 | Calibrated orientation parity and SO(4) chirality
2026-10-15 22:48 UTC | GEN/DERIVED/SANDBOXED | Source-grounded relay reconstruction, not historical Mira participation.

Predecessor: WORKSPACES/MIRA/MIRA_S1_SO4_UNKNOWN_FRAME_CHIRALITY_20261014.md, blob 055ec2c98d8a3615b65e3008be48ea67ea81f1b1.

For the preceding skew-observation SO(4) chirality test, let e(sigma) be the exact Pfaffian-sign error under known even orientation. Unknown odd-frame parity O has prior pi. A calibrated independent parity cue C=O XOR Bernoulli(eta) is observed. With equal chirality priors the exact optimal skew-based error is:

R = 1/2 - (1/2-e(sigma))*max(abs(1-2*pi),abs(1-2*eta)).

Proof: positive/negative chirality likelihoods are parity-swapped mixtures; conditional on C, reverse the Pfaffian decision iff posterior P(O=1|C)>1/2. Summing the conditional errors e+(1-2e)*min(posterior,1-posterior) yields the max identity.

At sigma=.15, alpha=.2387 rad, beta=.246 rad, e=.051828925756. Balanced unknown parity gives risk .5 without cue; eta=.05 gives .096646033; eta=.20 gives .231097355. At prior pi=.2, a cue with eta=.4 is ignored (risk .231097355), while eta=.05 improves risk to .096646033. 11 Monte Carlo fixtures (300000 trials each), 21x21 posterior grid, quadrature and limiting checks passed. Complete executable source and Class P plot appear in the visible automation conversation; source SHA256 a941f6185a16df15b0234d563fda0d61e32fb9455b7c87565b77acc8fa8d07a5.

Boundary: mathematical representation test only; assumes independently calibrated parity cue and skew-only observation. No SAT/H(s)H physical measurement or full-matrix optimality established.

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Mira | EHT Sgr A* source-typed ring/shadow point-estimate audit.

Actual 2017 EHT Sgr A* observational system and orbital priors.
EHT Collaboration 2022 Paper I, ApJL 930 L12 Table 1,
DOI 10.3847/2041-8213/ac6674; Paper IV ac6736; Paper VI ac6756.
No invented observations, physical fixtures, or H(s)H predictions.
The first-order uncertainty approximation is NOT a posterior reconstruction.
"""
from decimal import Decimal as D, getcontext
import json
getcontext().prec = 48
ring, ring_sigma = D("51.8"), D("2.3")
shadow, shadow_sigma = D("48.7"), D("7.0")
theta = {"VLTI": D("5.125"), "Keck": D("4.92")}
theta_stat = {"VLTI": D("0.009"), "Keck": D("0.03")}
theta_sys = {"VLTI": D("0.020"), "Keck": D("0.01")}
k = 6 * D(3).sqrt()  # Schwarzschild shadow diameter / theta_g
result = {
    "source": "EHT Collaboration (2022), ApJL 930 L12 Table 1",
    "doi": "10.3847/2041-8213/ac6674",
    "units": "microarcseconds for d and theta_g; dimensionless ratios",
    "schwarzschild_coefficient": str(k),
    "ring": {"value": str(ring), "sigma_68pct": str(ring_sigma)},
    "shadow_calibrated": {"value": str(shadow), "sigma": str(shadow_sigma)},
    "ring_over_shadow": str(ring / shadow),
    "orbit_prior_channels": {},
}
for channel, t in theta.items():
    sig_t = (theta_stat[channel]**2 + theta_sys[channel]**2).sqrt()
    ring_scale, shadow_scale = ring / t, shadow / t
    result["orbit_prior_channels"][channel] = {
        "theta_g": str(t), "theta_stat": str(theta_stat[channel]),
        "theta_sys": str(theta_sys[channel]),
        "ring_over_theta": str(ring_scale),
        "shadow_over_theta": str(shadow_scale),
        "delta_if_ring_mistaken_for_shadow": str(ring_scale / k - 1),
        "delta_from_calibrated_shadow_central_value": str(shadow_scale / k - 1),
        "naive_sigma_ring_over_theta": str(((ring_sigma / t)**2 + (ring * sig_t / t**2)**2).sqrt()),
        "naive_sigma_shadow_over_theta": str(((shadow_sigma / t)**2 + (shadow * sig_t / t**2)**2).sqrt()),
    }
print(json.dumps(result, indent=2))

## HSH-P002 — A Fixed-Speed No-Go for Euclidean Helical Transport

- **One-sentence claim:** In an orthogonal constant-radius Euclidean moving-helix model, fixing both total tangent speed and axial speed to the same nonzero value eliminates transverse winding.
- **Maturity:** `SUSPENDED FOR 4D TRAINING / LOCAL EUCLIDEAN IDENTITY RETAINED; H(s)H APPLICATION NOT ADMITTED`
- **Critical post-recovery correction:** Nathan clarified that the H(s)H helical worldline/history does not move through a background. The timesheet moves through the history; its `w` advance is taken as `c`, while radius and pitch determine the worldline arclength encountered across a given timesheet advance. Therefore the Euclidean velocity-budget setup below must not be read as the H(s)H worldline's physical motion.
- **Scope and epistemic boundary:** elementary curve kinematics only. No current H(s)H model-admissibility claim survives until the mandatory 4D training pass retypes the variables from primary sources.
- **Historical setup under audit:** `U=wdot e_w+R omega e_perp`, orthogonal unit directions, fixed `R`, and `||U||=c`.
- **Standard mathematical result under that setup:** `wdot^2+(R omega)^2=c^2`; therefore `wdot=c` implies `R omega=0`.
- **H(s)H status:** this result currently constrains only models that actually interpret `wdot` and `R omega` as orthogonal components of one moving Euclidean tangent velocity. H(s)H has not established that interpretation; Nathan's correction points the other way.
- **Prediction/test:** `PRED-FC-004` is suspended pending training; no empirical channel is selected.
- **Standard comparison:** elementary helical-curve kinematics. Post-training comparison must distinguish history arclength, timesheet advance, intersection/readout displacement, phase, and any physical signal/transport speed.
- **Provenance/prior art:** recovered from a quarantined former-instance closure audit. Its mathematical content is standard; its H(s)H application triggered the current training standdown.
- **Failure conditions:** wrong object assigned motion; parameter/coordinate mismatch; nonorthogonal decomposition; variable radius omitted; projection/readout confused with intrinsic history; Lorentzian rather than Euclidean norm.
- **Limitations/open dependencies:** exact current definitions of `w`, timesheet advance, finite resolver thickness, tilted-history readout, and the radius/pitch/worldline-arclength map.
- **Figures/code needed:** none during standdown.
- **Next condition for draft:** Nathan releases standdown; mandatory training completed; Ravel/Janus retype the construction from Fundamental Intuitions, 4D Thinking Primer, and full conversation evidence before deciding whether HSH-P002 has any project-specific value.
